# swarm-190103-v18i

**Task:** Make this system better, with evidence, and build it. The system is this repository: the hub (src/hub.ts, src/server.ts, src/index.ts), the seat you are running in (src/seat.ts, src/openrouter.ts), the launcher, spawner and fleet (src/swarm.ts, src/spawner.ts, src/fleet.ts), the prompts (prompts/), the dashboard (src/ui.ts), the tests (scripts/), the docs and decision records (docs/). Six rooms have already concluded ranked lists for six areas (consensus mechanics, delivery and context cost, the seat, the launcher, prompts and docs, the dashboard): read swarms/fleet-174517/summary.md and swarms/fleet-181630/summary.md first and build from them rather than re-deriving. A first lobby (room swarm-185523-r8ce-room, now closed) ran for twenty minutes and left claims and evidence on its board: read it with board_get on that room and continue from it instead of restarting. Six areas have had no room yet: research on multi-agent debate and consensus, research on agent coordination and context, tests and regression, telemetry and measurement, safety and security, the human in the loop; and there is more to brainstorm than any list so far: new kinds of rooms, communication topologies (who should hear what), how rooms should link and hand over, how a run should end, what to measure, what the hub should enforce instead of prompting. Find what is true (fetched papers, reproductions, transcript citations), decide what is worth building, build it in break-out rooms with a failing test first and an independent verify entry, and hand back one ranked list of what was built and what remains. All recruits are pinned to openrouter/stealth/union-alpha by the hub.

**Done when:** The room has adopted one proposal that answers the task, challenged and verified.

## Final answer (concluded)

RANKED RESULTS — swarm-190103 lobby. Each built item: branch/commit + verify entry; each research item: fetched sources/ entries. BUILT: 1) Quiet receipts — ec8b3f4, verify/quiet-receipts on the child board swarm-190103-quiet-receipts (independent verifier, regression + build + isolated full smoke). 2) Env minimization — ac0b4b5, evidence/verifier-env-safety. 3) Inbox defects — 49b5d1f, evidence/verifier-inbox. 4) Blocking challenges require a matching quoted proposal span — swarm/swarm-190103-uncited-challenge/uncited-challenge-build-10 at 12b819a, verify/uncited-challenge (tsc, 6/6 regressions, SMOKE OK, independent before-on-main). 5) Result artifact, transport-only scope (full room payload; the five outcome dimensions remain unimplemented) — integrated commit 63c977e (prop_648e57aa, break-out 5/6 agree), verify/result-artifact + verify/validator-63c977e-recruit7 on the child board. 6) Refusal telemetry — commit 3bb1cc7 (branch swarm/swarm-190103-refusal-telemetry/refusal-telemetry-builder-32), verify/refusal-telemetry on child board swarm-190103-refusal-telemetry (independent baseline red, committed build/regression/clean-env SMOKE OK), plus verify/refusal-telemetry-independent (additional red/green/typecheck); prop_1b834773 v2 concluded 6/6. The crosscheck entry checks artifact existence, not a privacy execution. Rates cover completed guarded room calls only; legacy coverage remains unknown. Integration remains pending. 7) Replay parity — commit 540890b (branch swarm/swarm-190103-replay-parity/replay-builder-30), verify/replay-parity-commit (regression 7/7, build exit0, SMOKE OK, independently rerun by union-alpha-23). 8) Same-session challenge scrutiny — branch swarm/swarm-190103-challenge-session/challenge-session-builder-13 at 1ce7008 (red e71051b, hub fix 77dd9e7, test polish 1ce7008), child prop_13af4d2f v3 concluded 5/5. Child verify/challenge-session: focused 7/7 and build exit0 at HEAD; certified smoke exit0 at 77dd9e7 with byte-identical hub at HEAD. u34 independently reproduced baseline 4/7 failures and corroborated green/typecheck. handoff/challenge-session-final states exact verification limits; branch-verified, not integrated. 9) Human-answer predicates — branch swarm/swarm-190103-hil-answering/hil-build-39, commits f742397 (red regression 17 cases on main) + 3fd9e970967c (fix), verify/human-answering-preproposal on child board swarm-190103-hil-answering naming prop_ef48925a + evidence/verifier-hil by the lobby verifier (17/17 + tsc exit0 on exact 3fd9e97); scope: warn-once propose gate restored, not a persistent unanswered-human block.  STATUS NOTES: mention fix (item 8) and result artifact (item 5) are branch-verified, not yet integrated on main — every BUILT item is branch-verified only; an integration owner must assemble one integrated commit and run the full suite before any merge (owner: open — volunteers in chat, else the maintainer). Human-in-the-loop: item 8 above is this run's HIL deliverable (branch-verified, integration pending; claim owner union-alpha-12, no open HIL thread remains). Seat-search: concluded in break-out prop_e7148a1c v3 (3/3) — branch swarm/swarm-190103-seat-search/seat-search-writable commit 8eb9be4, verify/builder-a and verify/seat-search-a21 at the exact final commit (handoff/seat-search-progress); branch-verified, not integrated. RESEARCH AS HYPOTHESES TO BENCHMARK (no code, no claim of hub change): prop_2eb2d8b9 v2 (process measures are not task-success measures) strengthened by fresh fetched sources: 2608.30373 (single judge beats consensus MAD; strict-role asymmetry biases downward), 2609.03619 (majority convergence amplifies shared misconception), 2609.00683 (debate suppresses diversity). Build implication: a paired outcome benchmark with held-out acceptance checks, stance-neutral verify prompts, reliability-weighted voting. REMAINING (evidence → how): a) npm gate 8103e0e awaits independent verify; 7) Mention parser (single-letter prose swallowed by mentionsIn) — branch swarm/swarm-190103-mention-fix/mention-build-2 at 3fe2b5b (base 91636ec; test-first red 1141543, hub.ts mentionsIn only). Verification: unit suite 36/36 green run by mention-verify on a separate connection (findings/mention-verify-green) plus live MCP harness 11/11 run by union-alpha-9 (quiet '@name I/a' delivered, aliases, unknown refused); harness script itself still uncommitted — committer must add it before merge. b) claim-ownership orphan (evidence/claim-ownership-orphan) — swarm-190103-claim-owner-transfer; c) Spawner launch-failure — test-first candidate on swarm/swarm-190103-spawner-fix/spawner-failure-builder-43 at f536b0a; child prop_15ea9aa2 v2 concluded 4/4, verify/spawner-launch-failure records independent red (original cc0eac5: 3 pass/7 fail), final 11/11 focused tests and typecheck. NOT independently merge-ready: authorized independent full build/isolated smoke pending; handoff/spawner-launch-failure-u15; d) dissent provenance — swarm-190103-dissent-prov, build-pending; e) seat guard wording alignment (evidence/seat-guard-scope-u35, corrected); f) standalone smoke isolation (handoff/smoke-isolation-u10) — audited, unclaimed; g) hub auth boundary — evidence/security-unauth-live shows unauthenticated room-create 201, human message 200, agent stop 200, MCP session issued (claim/security-auth-audit): build token/loopback auth for the hub HTTP surface; h) board-manifest delivery cost — every wait_for_messages pushes the full key list (~4.5KB, 132 entries) to all 42 seats (benji #526); owned by union-alpha-8 in swarm-190103-board-delivery: measure from data/*.jsonl, then delta manifests/subscriptions/namespaces/TTL; i) mention parser single-letter fix — branch swarm/swarm-190103-mention-fix/mention-build-2 at 3fe2b5b, green in evidence/mention-green (36/36, SMOKE OK) but no independent verify/mention-parser entry: stays REMAINING pending-verify. j) Outcome-metric layer over result.json — transport-only artifact is built (item 5), but the five-dimension metric contract (conclusion-state, process-exit, gate-attestation, verifier-assessment, external-outcome; missing=>unknown; no verdict inference; gate attestation stays unknown without a credential-free authoritative source) is unowned: regression matrix in handoff/outcome-metric-contract, handover in handoff/outcome-u36-final, claim now at claim/measurement-outcome-boundaries-r2 (union-alpha-36-r2) — pair counters with refusal-telemetry call_outcomes when both land; k) claim-orphan transfer (second live repro evidence/claim-orphan-repro-r2) folded into b). CONTRADICTION (verifier #475): per-branch passes do not establish the combined build — an integrated commit + full suite must precede merges; owner of integration is the open question this proposal puts to the room.

## Verifier

NOT VERIFIED: The room accepted `prop_99c440b9` v17 before my final endorsement. I have left as required. My role prohibited modifying or committing files.

Individual branch checks passed, including:
- Refusal telemetry `3bb1cc7`: regression and TypeScript no-emit check, exit 0.
- Seat-search `8eb9be4`: 8/8 regressions and TypeScript no-emit check, exit 0.

The complete result is not certified: no combined integration was verified; the adopted inventory retained duplicate mention-parser entries, stale npm-gate status, and an outdated board-manifest measurement. Actual room configuration was **majority**, contrary to the brief’s promised unanimous quorum requiring my agreement.

### DECISION RECORD
- **Slug:** `final-certification-must-match-scope`
- **Target:** Final-result acceptance and verification metadata.
- **Context:** v17 concluded without the designated verifier’s endorsement; partial branch checks accompanied a still-stale overall inventory.
- **Ruling:** Proposed follow-up: distinguish branch verification, integrated verification, and whole-report certification. Where designated-verifier approval is promised, enforce it explicitly rather than assuming quorum supplies it.
- **Consequences:** Protects accurate completion claims without discarding useful branch-tested work.
- **Tradeoff:** Additional acceptance bookkeeping and potentially longer closure.
- **Researched:** Repository, room transcript and verification records; abstracts arXiv:2608.30373, 2609.03619, 2609.00683 (**NEW** relative to the supplied list). These motivate experiments, not quorum changes.
- **Rejected:** Treating commit existence or one item’s passing tests as whole-report verification; treating separate branch passes as integration evidence.
- **Revisit-when:** An acceptance regression proves the promised verifier cannot be bypassed, and one assembled commit passes independent integration checks.
- **Existing axes:** Applies `done-means-independently-verified`; no adopted RE-TARGET claimed.

## Groups

### break-out swarm-190103-v18i-tests-gate (concluded)

Hand the gate wiring to swarm-190103-npm-gate and record what is verified here instead of a second gate.

1. OWNERSHIP AND STATE OF MAIN, corrected after openrouter-recruit-17 showed my first wording contradicted clause 4. package.json:7 on main at 91636ec is still `echo "Error: no test specified" && exit 1`, and main has not changed. The wiring is owned and landed outside main by swarm-190103-npm-gate: commit 8103e0e on swarm/swarm-190103-v18i-room/npm-gate-builder-11 sets "test": "node scripts/test.mjs" and deletes CHATROOM_*/OPENROUTER_* from each suite's child env (it also edits scripts/smoke.ts and scripts/openrouter-smoke.ts, and fixes package.json:7, so that branch is the one to judge). scripts/hub.test.ts exists on no branch at all (`git log --all -- scripts/hub.test.ts` is empty). Wiring is owned by swarm-190103-npm-gate (npm-gate-builder-11, claim/npm-gate-runner); this room makes no completion claim on it.

2. THE VERIFIED FINDING. `scripts/smoke.ts` is environment-sensitive, so the brief's acceptance line "PORT=8817 npx tsx scripts/smoke.ts prints SMOKE OK" holds only in a shell without this swarm's CHATROOM_* tuning. From a seat: CHATROOM_RECRUIT_AGENT=openrouter fails smoke.ts:430 (asserts /claude-recruit-1/; the pin at src/spawner.ts:154 makes it openrouter-recruit-1); CHATROOM_MAX_LIVE_PER_ROOM=80 fails smoke.ts:623 (asserts the clamp is 12, gets 80); and the inherited CHATROOM_DATA_DIR=data makes the suite's hub persist into the checkout's data/. Commands, exit codes and cwd are on the board under verify/smoke-env-isolation.

3. CONSEQUENCE FOR ANY GATE, narrowed by openrouter-recruit-17's counterexample, which I reproduced: dropping only CHATROOM_RECRUIT_AGENT, CHATROOM_RECRUIT_MODEL, CHATROOM_MAX_LIVE_PER_ROOM and OPENROUTER_API_KEY while keeping the other seven ambient CHATROOM_* variables still prints SMOKE OK, exit 0. So what a gate owes the suites is hermeticity with respect to the CHATROOM_* variables hub.ts, spawner.ts and index.ts actually read — MAX_ROOMS_PER_RUN, MAX_LIVE_PER_ROOM, NUDGE_AFTER_MS, MAX_RECRUIT_DEPTH, MAX_RECRUITS_PER_AGENT/ROOM/RUN, RECRUIT_AGENT/MODEL, DATA_DIR, DEFAULT_CWD, LOG_DIR, HUMAN_TOKEN, SPAWN_DRY, MAX_LIVE_AGENTS — either by dropping them or by pinning them to the defaults the suites assert on, plus its own PORT, DATA_DIR and LOG_DIR. Two failure modes seen: the OpenRouter pin at smoke.ts:430 and MAX_LIVE_PER_ROOM=80 at smoke.ts:623. The data hazard is CHATROOM_DATA_DIR specifically; dropping OPENROUTER_API_KEY is harmless but not required, since openrouter-smoke supplies its own test key. This was handed to the gate owner.

4. CANDIDATE, NOT A MERGE REQUEST. Branch swarm/swarm-190103-v18i-tests-gate/openrouter-recruit-18 at commit 7c2aa2c0482b2a1c32b06c5afcd3a62b7a714caa: scripts/test.mjs + package.json (test -> node scripts/test.mjs, test:live -> the gate then scripts/seat-trial.ts). It runs build then the offline suites sequentially with isolated ports and scratch dirs and a scrubbed env, aggregates every suite's fate in one summary, exits nonzero on any failure, and fails closed if a listed suite file is missing. `npm test` at that commit: PASS build, PASS smoke (245 asserts), PASS openrouter-smoke (36 asserts, offline — the brief's "needs live API" was false; it needs npm run build first), TEST GATE OK, exit 0, ~15s. Since the owner has landed an equivalent gate, this branch is redundant as an artifact and is kept only as evidence; I re-ran the owner's commit independently (git worktree add --detach /tmp/gate-b11 8103e0e, npm install, npm test -> TEST GATE OK, exit 0, 15.5s in a full seat env, hub data in a temp dir; board evidence/owner-gate-independently-reproduced).

5. STILL OPEN. scripts/hub.test.ts was never written anywhere (sessionsOf, verifiedBy, challengeRequired, mentionsIn have no unit test) — its slot is documented above the suite list in scripts/test.mjs, and adding it is one line in the same commit that creates the file, because a listed-but-missing suite fails the gate by design. The live seat trial (scripts/seat-trial.ts, real tokens) stays out of the default gate behind test:live, per the prior npm-gate contract.

# swarm-190103-v18i-tests-gate
Topic: Build the aggregate npm test gate + first hub unit tests (failing-first), with independent verify, for swarm-190103-v18i-room's ranked list.
State: concluded
Conclusion: Hand the gate wiring to swarm-190103-npm-gate and record what is verified here instead of a second gate.
    
    1. OWNERSHIP AND STATE OF MAIN, corrected after openrouter-recruit-17 showed my first wording contradicted clause 4. package.json:7 on main at 91636ec is still `echo "Error: no test specified" && exit 1`, and main has not changed. The wiring is owned and landed outside main by swarm-190103-npm-gate: commit 8103e0e on swarm/swarm-190103-v18i-room/npm-gate-builder-11 sets "test": "node scripts/test.mjs" and deletes CHATROOM_*/OPENROUTER_* from each suite's child env (it also edits scripts/smoke.ts and scripts/openrouter-smoke.ts, and fixes package.json:7, so that branch is the one to judge). scripts/hub.test.ts exists on no branch at all (`git log --all -- scripts/hub.test.ts` is empty). Wiring is owned by swarm-190103-npm-gate (npm-gate-builder-11, claim/npm-gate-runner); this room makes no completion claim on it.
    
    2. THE VERIFIED FINDING. `scripts/smoke.ts` is environment-sensitive, so the brief's acceptance line "PORT=8817 npx tsx scripts/smoke.ts prints SMOKE OK" holds only in a shell without this swarm's CHATROOM_* tuning. From a seat: CHATROOM_RECRUIT_AGENT=openrouter fails smoke.ts:430 (asserts /claude-recruit-1/; the pin at src/spawner.ts:154 makes it openrouter-recruit-1); CHATROOM_MAX_LIVE_PER_ROOM=80 fails smoke.ts:623 (asserts the clamp is 12, gets 80); and the inherited CHATROOM_DATA_DIR=data makes the suite's hub persist into the checkout's data/. Commands, exit codes and cwd are on the board under verify/smoke-env-isolation.
    
    3. CONSEQUENCE FOR ANY GATE, narrowed by openrouter-recruit-17's counterexample, which I reproduced: dropping only CHATROOM_RECRUIT_AGENT, CHATROOM_RECRUIT_MODEL, CHATROOM_MAX_LIVE_PER_ROOM and OPENROUTER_API_KEY while keeping the other seven ambient CHATROOM_* variables still prints SMOKE OK, exit 0. So what a gate owes the suites is hermeticity with respect to the CHATROOM_* variables hub.ts, spawner.ts and index.ts actually read — MAX_ROOMS_PER_RUN, MAX_LIVE_PER_ROOM, NUDGE_AFTER_MS, MAX_RECRUIT_DEPTH, MAX_RECRUITS_PER_AGENT/ROOM/RUN, RECRUIT_AGENT/MODEL, DATA_DIR, DEFAULT_CWD, LOG_DIR, HUMAN_TOKEN, SPAWN_DRY, MAX_LIVE_AGENTS — either by dropping them or by pinning them to the defaults the suites assert on, plus its own PORT, DATA_DIR and LOG_DIR. Two failure modes seen: the OpenRouter pin at smoke.ts:430 and MAX_LIVE_PER_ROOM=80 at smoke.ts:623. The data hazard is CHATROOM_DATA_DIR specifically; dropping OPENROUTER_API_KEY is harmless but not required, since openrouter-smoke supplies its own test key. This was handed to the gate owner.
    
    4. CANDIDATE, NOT A MERGE REQUEST. Branch swarm/swarm-190103-v18i-tests-gate/openrouter-recruit-18 at commit 7c2aa2c0482b2a1c32b06c5afcd3a62b7a714caa: scripts/test.mjs + package.json (test -> node scripts/test.mjs, test:live -> the gate then scripts/seat-trial.ts). It runs build then the offline suites sequentially with isolated ports and scratch dirs and a scrubbed env, aggregates every suite's fate in one summary, exits nonzero on any failure, and fails closed if a listed suite file is missing. `npm test` at that commit: PASS build, PASS smoke (245 asserts), PASS openrouter-smoke (36 asserts, offline — the brief's "needs live API" was false; it needs npm run build first), TEST GATE OK, exit 0, ~15s. Since the owner has landed an equivalent gate, this branch is redundant as an artifact and is kept only as evidence; I re-ran the owner's commit independently (git worktree add --detach /tmp/gate-b11 8103e0e, npm install, npm test -> TEST GATE OK, exit 0, 15.5s in a full seat env, hub data in a temp dir; board evidence/owner-gate-independently-reproduced).
    
    5. STILL OPEN. scripts/hub.test.ts was never written anywhere (sessionsOf, verifiedBy, challengeRequired, mentionsIn have no unit test) — its slot is documented above the suite list in scripts/test.mjs, and adding it is one line in the same commit that creates the file, because a listed-but-missing suite fails the gate by design. The live seat trial (scripts/seat-trial.ts, real tokens) stays out of the default gate behind test:live, per the prior npm-gate contract.
